<a id="words-2001-2100"></a>
## Words 2,001 – 2,100

<a id="w-2001"></a>
### 2001. [originally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/originally) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈrɪdʒənəli/` · **US:** `/əˈrɪdʒənəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ori/origi/originally__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ori/origi/originally__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्टतः, आदि में, शुरु शुरु में
- **Examples:**
  - The school was originally very small.
  - Originally, we had intended to go to Italy, but then we won the trip to Greece.
  - Laib originally planned to be a doctor.
  - She comes originally from York.

<a id="w-2002"></a>
### 2002. [orphan](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/orphan)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/orphan)
- **Meaning:** अनाथ करना, अनाथ, यतीम

<a id="w-2003"></a>
### 2003. [outcome](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outcome) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtkʌm/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtkʌm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outco/outcome__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outco/outcome__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परिणाम, नतीजा, निष्कर्ष
- **Examples:**
  - The likely outcome is a compromise.
  - Their strategy produced the desired outcome.
  - We are confident of a positive outcome.
  - a favourable/successful outcome

<a id="w-2004"></a>
### 2004. [outdoor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outdoor) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtdɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtdɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outdo/outdoor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outdo/outdoor__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाहरी, बाह्य, खुली हवा में
- **Examples:**
  - outdoor pursuits/recreation/activities
  - They sell camping equipment, hiking gear and outdoor clothing.
  - an outdoor swimming pool
  - I'm not really the outdoor type (= I prefer indoor activities).

<a id="w-2005"></a>
### 2005. [outer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outer) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outer/outer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outer/outer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाहरी, सुदूर, बाह्य
- **Examples:**
  - the outer layers of the skin
  - While the chassis is made of steel, the outer shell is plastic.
  - The attackers began to enter through the outer walls.
  - The stair winds up between the inner and outer walls of the house.

<a id="w-2006"></a>
### 2006. [outline](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outline_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtlaɪn/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtlaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outli/outline__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outli/outline__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रूपरेखा, खाका, सारांश
- **Examples:**
  - outline of something This is a brief outline of the events.
  - The broad outlines of Marley's life are relatively well known.
  - You should draw up a plan or outline for the essay.
  - You need a written outline of the story.

<a id="w-2007"></a>
### 2007. [outlook](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outlook) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtlʊk/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtlʊk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outlo/outlook__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outlo/outlook__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दृष्टिकोन, दृष्टिकोण, दृश्य
- **Examples:**
  - Travel broadens your outlook.
  - a/an positive/optimistic outlook
  - a bleak/gloomy/pessimistic outlook
  - outlook on something He had a practical outlook on life.

<a id="w-2008"></a>
### 2008. [output](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/output_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtpʊt/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtpʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outpu/output__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outpu/output__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्पादन, प्रक्षेपण, आउटपुट
- **Examples:**
  - Manufacturing output has increased by 8 per cent.

<a id="w-2009"></a>
### 2009. [outrage](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outrage_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊtreɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊtreɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/out/outra/outrage__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/out/outra/outrage__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुत खराब बात, नाराज़गी, हिंसात्मक  कार्य
- **Examples:**
  - The judge's remarks caused public outrage.
  - She was filled with a strong sense of moral outrage.
  - Environmentalists have expressed outrage at the ruling.

<a id="w-2010"></a>
### 2010. [outset](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outset)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/outset)
- **Meaning:** प्रारंभ, शुरुवात से ही

<a id="w-2011"></a>

- **UK:** `/ˈpæsɪndʒə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpæsɪndʒər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/passe/passenger__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/passe/passenger__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कर्मी दल, याट्री, पथिक
- **Examples:**
  - airline/cruise/rail/bus passengers
  - I soon got talking to a fellow passenger on the flight.
  - The boat was carrying more than 100 passengers.
  - a passenger train (= carrying passengers, not goods)

<a id="w-2046"></a>
### 2046. [passion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/passion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpæʃn/` · **US:** `/ˈpæʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/passi/passion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/passi/passion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इश्क, क्रोध, धुन
- **Examples:**
  - I admire the passion and commitment shown by the players.
  - with passion She argued her case with considerable passion.
  - a crime of passion (= caused by strong feelings of sexual jealousy)
  - Passions were running high (= people were angry and emotional) at the meeting.

### 2047. [passionate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/passionate) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpæʃənət/` · **US:** `/ˈpæʃənət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/passi/passionate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/passi/passionate__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-2048"></a>
### 2048. [passive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/passive_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpæsɪv/` · **US:** `/ˈpæsɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/passi/passive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/passi/passive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कर्मवाच्य, निष्क्रीय, कर्मप्रधन

<a id="w-2052"></a>
### 2052. [patent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/patent_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpeɪtnt/` · **US:** `/ˈpætnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pat/paten/patent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pat/paten/patent__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - patent on something to apply for/obtain/take out a patent on an invention
  - by patent The device was protected by patent.
  - patent applications/laws
  - the US Patent Office

<a id="w-2053"></a>
### 2053. [pathway](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pathway) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɑːθweɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈpæθweɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pat/pathw/pathway__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pat/pathw/pathway__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पथ
- **Examples:**

<a id="w-2054"></a>
### 2054. [patience](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/patience) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpeɪʃns/` · **US:** `/ˈpeɪʃns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pat/patie/patience__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pat/patie/patience__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संयम, धैर्य, सहनशीलता
- **Examples:**
  - She has little patience with (= will not accept or consider) such views.
  - People have lost patience with (= have become annoyed about) the slow pace of reform.
  - I have run out of patience with her.
  - My patience is wearing thin (= I do not have much patience left).

<a id="w-2055"></a>
### 2055. [patrol](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/patrol_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈtrəʊl/` · **US:** `/pəˈtrəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pat/patro/patrol__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pat/patro/patrol__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - on patrol a police car on patrol

<a id="w-2056"></a>
### 2056. [patron](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/patron) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpeɪtrən/` · **US:** `/ˈpeɪtrən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pat/patro/patron__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pat/patro/patron__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संरक्षक, दर्शक, ग्राहक
- **Examples:**

  - He was a great patron of the arts and helped to establish the Baltimore Museum.

<a id="w-2057"></a>
### 2057. [pause](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pause_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pɔːz/` · **US:** `/pɔːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pau/pause/pause__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pau/pause/pause__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विराम, ठहराव, रोक
- **Examples:**
  - There was a long pause before she answered.
  - After a brief pause, they continued climbing.
  - pause in something David waited for a pause in the conversation so he could ask his question.
  - without pause The rain fell without pause.

- **UK:** `/ˈpeɪmənt/` · **US:** `/ˈpeɪmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pay/payme/payment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pay/payme/payment__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-2061"></a>
### 2061. [peak](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/peak_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/piːk/` · **US:** `/piːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pea/peak_/peak__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pea/peak_/peak__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चोटी, अधिकतम, टोपी का किनारा
- **Examples:**
  - Traffic reaches its peak between 8 and 9 in the morning.

  - at the peak of something She's at the peak of her career.
  - Membership of the club has fallen from a peak of 600 people in 2006.

### 2062. [peasant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/peasant) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** असभ्य, देहाती, किसान

<a id="w-2063"></a>
### 2063. [peculiar](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/peculiar) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pɪˈkjuːliə(r)/` · **US:** `/pɪˈkjuːliər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pec/pecul/peculiar__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pec/pecul/peculiar__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निराला, असामान्य, विशेष
- **Examples:**
  - a peculiar smell/taste
  - The meat tasted rather peculiar.
  - There was something peculiar in the way he smiled.
  - I had a peculiar feeling we'd met before.

<a id="w-2064"></a>
### 2064. [peer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/peer_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/pɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pee/peer_/peer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pee/peer_/peer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जोड़, सामंत, समकक्ष  व्यक्ति
- **Examples:**
  - She enjoys the respect of her peers.
  - A peer review system is being introduced to help teachers who are experiencing difficulty.
  - Children are worried about failing in front of their peers.
  - Peer pressure is strong among young people (= they want to be like other people of the same age).

<a id="w-2065"></a>
### 2065. [penalty](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/penalty) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpenəlti/` · **US:** `/ˈpenəlti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pen/penal/penalty__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pen/penal/penalty__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दंड, दण्ड/सज़ा, पेनाल्ट्य्
- **Examples:**
  - to impose a penalty
  - Assault carries a maximum penalty of seven years' imprisonment.
  - penalty for (doing) something The penalty for travelling without a ticket is £200.
  - Contractors who fall behind schedule incur heavy financial penalties.

<a id="w-2066"></a>
### 2066. [perceive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perceive) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈsiːv/` · **US:** `/pərˈsiːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perce/perceive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perce/perceive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अर्थ लगाना, देखना, महसूस करना
- **Examples:**
  - perceive somebody/something/yourself (as something) This discovery was perceived as a major breakthrough.
  - I do not perceive myself as an expert.
  - A science degree and artistic interests are often perceived as incompatible.
  - perceive somebody/something to be/have something They were widely perceived to have been unlucky.

<a id="w-2067"></a>
### 2067. [perception](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perception) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈsepʃn/` · **US:** `/pərˈsepʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perce/perception__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perce/perception__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवबोधन, अभिज्ञता, अनुभूति
- **Examples:**
  - a campaign to change public perception of the police
  - perception that… There is a general public perception that standards in schools are falling.

<a id="w-2068"></a>
### 2068. [percentage](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/percentage) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈsentɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/pərˈsentɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perce/percentage__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perce/percentage__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अंश, अनुपात, प्रतिशतता
- **Examples:**
  - percentage of something/somebody What percentage of the population is/are overweight?
  - A high percentage of the female staff are part-time workers.
  - a large/small/low percentage
  - as a percentage The figure is expressed as a percentage.

<a id="w-2069"></a>
### 2069. [perfect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perfect_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːfɪkt/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrfɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perfe/perfect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perfe/perfect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूतकाल सम्बन्धी, आदर्श, ही
- **Examples:**
  - in perfect condition
  - He smiled, revealing a perfect set of teeth.
  - Well I'm sorry—but nobody's perfect (= used when somebody has criticized you).
  - In a perfect world, everybody would have everything they needed.

<a id="w-2070"></a>
### 2070. [perfectly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perfectly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːfɪktli/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrfɪktli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perfe/perfectly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perfe/perfectly__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सितारा मछली, पूर्णतः, अच्छी तरह से
- **Examples:**
  - It's perfectly normal to feel like this.
  - It's perfectly good as it is (= it doesn't need changing).
  - I thought he'd be upset, but he seems perfectly fine.
  - You know perfectly well what I mean.

<a id="w-2071"></a>
### 2071. [perform](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perform) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈfɔːm/` · **US:** `/pərˈfɔːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perfo/perform__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perfo/perform__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, दिखाना, गाना
- **Examples:**
  - to perform a song/dance/play
  - The play was first performed in 2007.
  - Visiting artists will perform their music and poetry.
  - I'm looking forward to seeing you perform.

<a id="w-2072"></a>
### 2072. [performance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/performance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈfɔːməns/` · **US:** `/pərˈfɔːrməns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perfo/performance__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perfo/performance__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कार्य, प्रदर्शन, क्रिया
- **Examples:**
  - The performance starts at seven.
  - an evening performance
  - They put on performances for the tourists.
  - This was one of the band's rare live performances.

<a id="w-2074"></a>
### 2074. [permanent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/permanent_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːmənənt/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrmənənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perma/permanent__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perma/permanent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थायी, सदा के लिए, कृत्रिम  लट
- **Examples:**
  - She was unable to find a permanent job.
  - Holiday camps employ only a very small number of permanent staff.
  - They are now living together on a permanent basis.
  - The accident has not done any permanent damage.

<a id="w-2075"></a>
### 2075. [permanently](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/permanently) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːmənəntli/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrmənəntli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perma/permanently__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perma/permanently__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थायी रूप से
- **Examples:**
  - The stroke left his right side permanently damaged.
  - She had decided to settle permanently in France.
  - This door is kept permanently locked.

<a id="w-2076"></a>
### 2076. [permission](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/permission) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈmɪʃn/` · **US:** `/pərˈmɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/permi/permission__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/permi/permission__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आज्ञा, सम्मति
- **Examples:**
  - permission for something You must ask permission for all major expenditure.
  - permission to do something After much persuasion, permission was granted to speak to the refugees at the camp.
  - The school has been refused permission to expand.
  - All minors must seek parental permission to marry.

<a id="w-2077"></a>
### 2077. [permit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/permit_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːmɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrmɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/permi/permit__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/permi/permit__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुमति, अनूज्ञा, आज्ञा
- **Examples:**
  - a parking/building permit
  - to apply for a permit
  - permit for something The agency issued permits for exploratory drilling.
  - The company failed to obtain the necessary permits for the landfill project.

<a id="w-2078"></a>
### 2078. [persist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/persist) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈsɪst/` · **US:** `/pərˈsɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/persi/persist__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/persi/persist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** डटे रहना, बना रहना, डटे  रहना
- **Examples:**
  - persist in doing something Why do you persist in blaming yourself for what happened?
  - persist in something She persisted in her search for the truth.
  - persist with something He persisted with his questioning.
  - + speech ‘So, did you agree or not?’ he persisted.

<a id="w-2079"></a>
### 2079. [persistent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/persistent) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈsɪstənt/` · **US:** `/pərˈsɪstənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/persi/persistent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/persi/persistent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सतत, लगातार, शाश्वत
- **Examples:**
  - How do you deal with persistent salesmen who won't take no for an answer?
  - a persistent offender (= a person who continues to commit crimes after they have been caught and punished)
  - She can be very persistent when she wants something.

- **Meaning:** खुशी, हर्ष, सूख
- **Examples:**
  - His eyes lit up with pleasure.
  - for pleasure Encourage your child to read for pleasure.
  - pleasure in something He takes no pleasure in his work.
  - pleasure in doing something She took pleasure in shocking her parents.

<a id="w-2112"></a>
### 2112. [pledge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pledge_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pledʒ/` · **US:** `/pledʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/ple/pledg/pledge__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/ple/pledg/pledge__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वचन, प्रतिज्ञा, वादा
- **Examples:**
  - pledge (of something) a pledge of support
  - pledge to do something Will the government honour its election pledge not to raise taxes?

  - pledge that… Management has made a pledge that there will be no job losses this year.

### 2132. [positive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/positive_1) *(adjective)*

- **Examples:**

  - The contributions have a positive impact on the lives of hundreds of children.
  - Overseas investment has had a positive effect on exports.
  - His family have been a very positive influence on him.

- **Examples:**
  - He was charged with possessing a shotgun without a licence.

  - Belgium was the first European country to possess a fully fledged rail network.

<a id="w-2135"></a>
### 2135. [possession](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/possession) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** आधिपत्य, अधिकार, सम्पत्ति
- **Examples:**

### 2137. [postpone](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/postpone) *(verb)*

- **Meaning:** आगे बढाना, स्थगित करना

  - postpone something to something They have agreed to postpone repayment of the loan to a future unspecified date.
  - postpone doing something It was an unpopular decision to postpone building the new hospital.

<a id="w-2146"></a>
### 2146. [precede](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precede) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsiːd/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsiːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prece/precede__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prece/precede__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आगे करना, आगे  करना, से पहले आना या घटित होना
- **Examples:**
  - the years preceding the war
  - His resignation was preceded by weeks of speculation.
  - She preceded me in the job.
  - in the moments which immediately preceded the earthquake

<a id="w-2147"></a>
### 2147. [precedent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precedent) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpresɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈpresɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prece/precedent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prece/precedent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उदाहरण, पूर्विका, परंपरागत तरीका
- **Examples:**
  - The ruling set a precedent for future libel cases.

<a id="w-2148"></a>
### 2148. [precise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precise) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsaɪs/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsaɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preci/precise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preci/precise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठीक, स्पष्ट, नियत
- **Examples:**
  - precise details/instructions/measurements
  - Can you give a more precise definition of the word?
  - I can be reasonably precise about the time of the incident.

<a id="w-2149"></a>
### 2149. [precisely](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precisely) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsaɪsli/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsaɪsli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preci/precisely__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preci/precisely__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शुद्ध  रुप  से, स्वभाववश, ठिक ठिक
- **Examples:**
  - They look precisely the same to me.
  - That's precisely what I meant.
  - It's not clear precisely how the accident happened.
  - The meeting starts at 2 o'clock precisely.

<a id="w-2150"></a>
### 2150. [precision](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precision) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsɪʒn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preci/precision__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preci/precision__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** यथार्थता, सूक्ष्मतामापी, परिशुद्धता/यथार्थता
- **Examples:**
  - Her writing is imaginative but lacks precision.
  - with precision He chose his words with precision.
  - done with mathematical precision
  - Historians can’t estimate the date with any (degree of) precision.

<a id="w-2151"></a>
### 2151. [predator](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predator) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpredətə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpredətər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preda/predator__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preda/predator__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लूट मार करने वाला, परभक्षी, लुटेरा
- **Examples:**
  - Some animals have no natural predators.
  - the relationship between predator and prey

<a id="w-2152"></a>
### 2152. [predecessor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predecessor) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpriːdəsesə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpredəsesər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prede/predecessor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prede/predecessor__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वज, पूर्ववर्ती, पूर्व अधिकारी
- **Examples:**
  - The new president reversed many of the policies of his predecessor.
  - his immediate predecessor in the post
  - my predecessor at the Ministry of Defence

<a id="w-2153"></a>
### 2153. [predict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predict) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪkt/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/predict__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/predict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वानुमान करना, भविष्यवाणी करना, भविष्य बतलाना
- **Examples:**
  - predict something a reliable method of predicting earthquakes
  - Nobody could predict the outcome.
  - to predict the future
  - He predicts a bright future for the business in the next financial year.

<a id="w-2154"></a>
### 2154. [predictable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predictable) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪktəbl/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪktəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/predictable__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/predictable__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वानुमेय होने के कारण  नीरस, पूर्वानुमेय, पूर्वानुमेय होने के कारण नीरस
- **Examples:**
  - a predictable result
  - The ending of the book was entirely predictable.
  - The disease follows a highly predictable pattern.
  - In March and April, the weather is much less predictable.

<a id="w-2155"></a>
### 2155. [prediction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prediction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪkʃn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/prediction__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/prediction__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भविष्य कथन, भविष्यवाणी
- **Examples:**
  - The results of the experiment confirmed our predictions.
  - prediction about/on something I've learned not to make predictions about the weather.
  - It's difficult to make accurate predictions about the effects on the environment.
  - Experts preview the game and offer their prediction on the outcome.

- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predominant)

### 2157. [preface](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preface)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preface)
- **Meaning:** _रारंभ होना या करना, प्रारम्भ करना, प्रस्तावना

<a id="w-2158"></a>
### 2158. [prefer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prefer) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈfɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/prɪˈfɜːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prefe/prefer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prefe/prefer__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पसन्द करना, तरक्की देना, पसंद करना
- **Examples:**
  - prefer something ‘Coffee or tea?’ ‘I'd prefer tea, thanks.’
  - I would prefer it if you didn't tell anyone.
  - A local firm is to be preferred.
  - A short-term fixed rate is the preferred option.

<a id="w-2159"></a>
### 2159. [preference](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preference) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprefrəns/` · **US:** `/ˈprefrəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prefe/preference__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prefe/preference__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्राथमिकता, पसंद, वरीयता
- **Examples:**
  - It's a matter of personal preference.
  - I can't say that I have any particular preference.
  - preference for somebody/something Many people expressed a strong preference for the original plan.
  - Let's make a list of possible speakers, in order of preference.

<a id="w-2160"></a>
### 2160. [pregnancy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pregnancy) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpreɡnənsi/` · **US:** `/ˈpreɡnənsi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/pregn/pregnancy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/pregn/pregnancy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गर्भावस्था, गर्भ
- **Examples:**
  - a pregnancy test
  - during pregnancy Many women experience sickness during pregnancy.
  - unplanned/unwanted pregnancies
  - the increase in teenage pregnancies

<a id="w-2161"></a>
### 2161. [pregnant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pregnant) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpreɡnənt/` · **US:** `/ˈpreɡnənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/pregn/pregnant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/pregn/pregnant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सारगर्भित, अर्थपूर्ण, उर्वर
- **Examples:**
  - My wife is pregnant.
  - pregnant with… I was pregnant with our third child at the time.
  - a heavily pregnant woman (= one whose baby is nearly ready to be born)
  - to get/become/fall pregnant

<a id="w-2162"></a>
### 2162. [prejudice](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prejudice_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpredʒədɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈpredʒədɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preju/prejudice__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preju/prejudice__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वाग्रह, पूर्वागह
- **Examples:**
  - a victim of racial prejudice
  - Their decision was based on ignorance and prejudice.
  - prejudice against somebody/something There was often prejudice against people with disabilities.
  - prejudice in favour of somebody/something She admitted to a prejudice in favour of British universities.

<a id="w-2163"></a>
### 2163. [preliminary](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preliminary_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈlɪmɪnəri/` · **US:** `/prɪˈlɪmɪneri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preli/preliminary__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preli/preliminary__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रारंभिक, प्रारंभिक दौर, प्रारंभिक तैयारी
- **Examples:**
  - After a few preliminary remarks he announced the winners.
  - preliminary results/findings/enquiries
  - the preliminary rounds of the contest
  - preliminary to something pilot studies preliminary to a full-scale study

### 2165. [premier](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/premier_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpremiə(r)/` · **US:** `/prɪˈmɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/premi/premier__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/premi/premier__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रमुख, प्रधानमंट्री, प्रधान
- **Examples:**
  - one of the country’s premier chefs
  - (British English, sport) the Premier League/Division

<a id="w-2166"></a>
### 2166. [premise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/premise) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpremɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈpremɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/premi/premise__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/premi/premise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिज्ञा, आधार  वाक्य
- **Examples:**
  - the basic premise of her argument
  - The argument rests on a false premise.
  - His reasoning is based on the premise that all people are equally capable of good and evil.
  - The premise on which they worked was that there would always be enough natural resources to support them.

<a id="w-2167"></a>
### 2167. [preparation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preparation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌprepəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌprepəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prepa/preparation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prepa/preparation__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्पाक, अभ्यासकार्य, म्पाक
- **Examples:**
  - food preparation
  - preparation for something Preparation for the party started early.
  - Careful preparation for the exam is essential.
  - No athlete would dream of entering a big race without adequate preparation.

<a id="w-2168"></a>
### 2168. [prescribe](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prescribe) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈskraɪb/` · **US:** `/prɪˈskraɪb/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presc/prescribe__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presc/prescribe__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्धारित करना, नुस्खा लिखना
- **Examples:**
  - prescribe something Valium is usually prescribed to treat anxiety.
  - prescribe (somebody) something (for something) He may be able to prescribe you something for that cough.

<a id="w-2169"></a>
### 2169. [prescription](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prescription) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presc/prescription__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presc/prescription__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नुस्खा, निर्धारित औषधि, चश्मे के लिये निर्देश
- **Examples:**
  - prescription for something The doctor gave me a prescription for antibiotics.
  - on prescription (British English) Antibiotics are only available on prescription.
  - by prescription (North American English) Antibiotics are only available by prescription.
  - They are not available without a prescription.

<a id="w-2170"></a>
### 2170. [presence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprezns/` · **US:** `/ˈprezns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/presence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/presence__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** के सामने, व्यक्तित्व, उपस्थिति
- **Examples:**
  - He hardly seemed to notice my presence.
  - Her presence during the crisis had a calming effect.
  - (formal) Your presence is requested at the meeting.
  - in the presence of somebody They married in the presence of their family members.

<a id="w-2171"></a>
### 2171. [presentation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presentation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌpreznˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌpriːznˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/presentation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/presentation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदर्शन, व्याख्यान, उपहारअ
- **Examples:**
  - presentation on/about somebody/something The sales manager will give a presentation on the new products.
  - Several speakers will be making short presentations.
  - The conference will begin with a keynote presentation by a leading industry figure.
  - a slide/video/multimedia presentation

<a id="w-2172"></a>
### 2172. [preserve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preserve_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈzɜːv/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzɜːrv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/preserve__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/preserve__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्भाल कर  रखना, बचाना, सुरक्षित रखना
- **Examples:**
  - He was anxious to preserve his reputation.
  - Efforts to preserve the peace have failed.
  - She managed to preserve her sense of humour under very trying circumstances.
  - He was insistent on preserving the integrity of the brand.

<a id="w-2173"></a>
### 2173. [preservation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preservation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌprezəˈveɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌprezərˈveɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/preservation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/preservation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संरक्षण, परिरक्षित फल या सुरक्षित स्थान, परिरक्षण
- **Examples:**
  - building/environmental/food preservation
  - a preservation group/society
  - The council has placed a preservation order on the building.

<a id="w-2174"></a>
### 2174. [presidency](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presidency) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprezɪdənsi/` · **US:** `/ˈprezɪdənsi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presi/presidency__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presi/presidency__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अध्यक्ष  पद, अध्यक्ष पद, अध्यक्षता
- **Examples:**

<a id="w-2176"></a>
### 2176. [presumably](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presumably) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈzjuːməbli/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzuːməbli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presu/presumably__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presu/presumably__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्भवतः, संभाव्यतः
- **Examples:**
  - Presumably this is where the accident happened.
  - You'll be taking the car, presumably?
  - I couldn't concentrate, presumably because I was so tired.
  - Presumably you’ll be leaving the children at home.

<a id="w-2177"></a>
### 2177. [presume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presume) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈzjuːm/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presu/presume__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presu/presume__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मान लेना, चुनौती देना, साहस करना
- **Examples:**
  - They are very expensive, I presume?
  - ‘Is he still abroad?’ ‘I presume so.’
  - presume (that)… I presumed (that) he understood the rules.
  - it is presumed that… Little is known of the youngest son; it is presumed that he died young.

<a id="w-2178"></a>
### 2178. [pretend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pretend_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈtend/` · **US:** `/prɪˈtend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prete/pretend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prete/pretend__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दावा करना, अभिनय करना, मान लेना
- **Examples:**
  - I'm tired of having to pretend all the time.
  - Of course I was wrong; it would be hypocritical to pretend otherwise.
  - pretend to somebody that… He pretended to his family that everything was fine.
  - pretend (that)… We pretended (that) nothing had happened.

<a id="w-2179"></a>
### 2179. [prevail](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prevail) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈveɪl/` · **US:** `/prɪˈveɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preva/prevail__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preva/prevail__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रचलित होना, जीत लेना
- **Examples:**
  - prevail in something We were horrified at the conditions prevailing in local prisons.
  - prevail among somebody Those beliefs still prevail among certain social groups.

<a id="w-2180"></a>
### 2180. [prevention](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prevention) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈvenʃn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈvenʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preve/prevention__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preve/prevention__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रोकथाम, रोक थाम
- **Examples:**
  - accident/crime prevention
  - the prevention of disease
  - a fire prevention officer
  - a drug prevention programme

<a id="w-2181"></a>
### 2181. [previous](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/previous) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpriːviəs/` · **US:** `/ˈpriːviəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/previ/previous__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/previ/previous__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पिछला, पूर्व, पूर्ववर्ती
- **Examples:**
  - No previous experience is necessary for this job.
  - The car has only had one previous owner.
  - She is his daughter from a previous marriage.
  - I was unable to attend because of a previous engagement.

<a id="w-2182"></a>
### 2182. [previously](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/previously) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpriːviəsli/` · **US:** `/ˈpriːviəsli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/previ/previously__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/previ/previously__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पहले
- **Examples:**
  - The building had previously been used as a hotel.
  - As previously reported, the couple separated in May.
  - The book contains a number of photographs not previously published.
  - I had visited them three days previously.

- **Meaning:** पूर्व, पूर्ववर्ती, मठाधिकारी

- **UK:** `/prəˈsiːdʒə(r)/` · **US:** `/prəˈsiːdʒər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/proce/procedure__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/proce/procedure__us_3.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - emergency/safety/disciplinary procedures

  - The school in this case did not follow the correct procedure.
  - to follow normal/standard/the proper procedure

<a id="w-2202"></a>
### 2202. [proceeding](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/proceeding) *(noun)*

  - bankruptcy/divorce/extradition proceedings

### 2205. [proclaim](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/proclaim) *(verb)*

- **Meaning:** कामचलाऊ ढंग से, कामचलाऊ  ढंग से, पर्याप्त माट्रा  में
- **Examples:**
  - The instructions are reasonably straightforward.
  - She seems reasonably happy in her new job.

### 2325. [reasoning](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reasoning) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈriːzənɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈriːzənɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reaso/reasoning__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reaso/reasoning__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - This line of reasoning is faulty.

<a id="w-2326"></a>
### 2326. [reassure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reassure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌriːəˈʃɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ˌriːəˈʃʊr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reass/reassure__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reass/reassure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आश्वासन देना, फिर हिम्मत दिलाना, आश्वासन  देना
- **Examples:**
  - reassure somebody (about something) They tried to reassure her, but she still felt anxious.
  - reassure somebody that… The doctor reassured him that there was nothing seriously wrong.
  - be reassured of something She needed to be reassured of his love for her.

### 2370. [regret](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/regret_2) *(noun)*

### 2373. [regulate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/regulate) *(verb)*

- **Meaning:** ठीक करना, नियन्ट्रित करना, नियंत्रित करना
- **Examples:**
  - The department is responsible for regulating the insurance industry.
  - The activities of credit companies are regulated by law.
  - The government accepts whaling in principle as long as it is carefully regulated.
  - It is up to the regulating authority to put the measures into effect.

### 2400. [relief](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/relief) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈliːf/` · **US:** `/rɪˈliːf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rel/relie/relief__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rel/relie/relief__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उभार, राहत, मुक्ति
- **Examples:**
  - I felt a huge sense of relief when I heard they were all OK.
  - We all breathed a sigh of relief when he left.
  - Much to my relief the car was not damaged.
  - News of their safety came as a great relief.

### 2401. [relieve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/relieve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈliːv/` · **US:** `/rɪˈliːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rel/relie/relieve__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rel/relie/relieve__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कम करना, सहायता करना, उठाना
- **Examples:**
  - to relieve the symptoms of a cold
  - to relieve anxiety/guilt/stress
  - Being able to tell the truth at last seemed to relieve her.

<a id="w-2408"></a>
### 2408. [remark](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remark_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmɑːk/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmɑːrk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remar/remark__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remar/remark__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-2409"></a>
### 2409. [remarkable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remarkable) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmɑːkəbl/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmɑːrkəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remar/remarkable__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remar/remarkable__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विलक्षण, विशिष्ट, उल्लेखनीय
- **Examples:**
  - a remarkable achievement/career/talent
  - She was a truly remarkable woman.
  - What is even more remarkable about the whole thing is…
  - remarkable for something The area is remarkable for its scenery.

<a id="w-2410"></a>
### 2410. [remarkably](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remarkably) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmɑːkəbli/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmɑːrkəbli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remar/remarkably__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remar/remarkably__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उल्लेखनीयता से, आश्चर्यजनक ढंग से, आश्चर्यजनक  ढंग से
- **Examples:**
  - The car is in remarkably good condition for its age.
  - Remarkably, nobody was killed.
  - The two reports are remarkably similar.

<a id="w-2411"></a>
### 2411. [remedy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remedy_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈremədi/` · **US:** `/ˈremədi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remed/remedy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remed/remedy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपाय, रोग का निवारण, दवा
- **Examples:**
  - When the reservoir becomes blocked, the only remedy lies in cleaning the entire system.
  - remedy for something There is no simple remedy for unemployment.
  - remedy to something There are a number of possible remedies to this problem.

<a id="w-2412"></a>
### 2412. [remind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remind) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmaɪnd/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmaɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remin/remind__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remin/remind__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** याद  दिलाना, चेताना, याद दिलाना
- **Examples:**
  - remind somebody/yourself I'm sorry, I've forgotten your name. Can you remind me?
  - That (= what you have just said, done, etc.) reminds me, I must get some cash.
  - ‘You need to finish that essay.’ ‘Don't remind me (= I don't want to think about it).’
  - remind somebody/yourself about/of something ‘Don't forget the camera.’ ‘Remind me about it nearer the time.’

<a id="w-2413"></a>
### 2413. [reminder](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reminder) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmaɪndə(r)/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmaɪndər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remin/reminder__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remin/reminder__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्मरण पत्र, तकाज़ा, अनुस्मारक
- **Examples:**
  - reminder of something The sheer size of the cathedral is a constant reminder of the power of religion.
  - The incident served as a timely reminder of just how dangerous mountaineering can be.
  - reminder that… The article concludes with a chilling reminder that the disease can strike anyone at any time.

<a id="w-2431"></a>
### 2431. [representative](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/representative_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌreprɪˈzentətɪv/` · **US:** `/ˌreprɪˈzentətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rep/repre/representative__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rep/repre/representative__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रातिनिधिक
- **Examples:**

  - The paper-thin models in magazines are not representative of most women.
  - Some residents argued that the protesters were not representative of the community.
  - The painting is not representative of his work of the period.

<a id="w-2438"></a>
### 2438. [reputation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reputation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌrepjuˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌrepjuˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rep/reput/reputation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rep/reput/reputation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिष्ठा, मर्यादा, मान
- **Examples:**
  - to have a good/bad reputation
  - to build/earn a reputation
  - reputation as something She soon established a reputation as a first-class cook.
  - reputation for something The company enjoys an international reputation for quality of design.

<a id="w-2439"></a>
### 2439. [request](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/request_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈkwest/` · **US:** `/rɪˈkwest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/req/reque/request__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/req/reque/request__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आवेदन, विनती, अनुरोध
- **Examples:**
  - request for something a request for information
  - They made a request for further aid.
  - request to do something He submitted a request to adopt a flexible working routine.
  - to grant a request

<a id="w-2440"></a>
### 2440. [requirement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/requirement) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈkwaɪəmənt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈkwaɪərmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/req/requi/requirement__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/req/requi/requirement__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जरुरत, आवश्यक्ता, आवश्यकता
- **Examples:**
  - the basic requirements of life
  - a software solution to meet your requirements

  - Our immediate requirement is extra staff.

### 2441. [rescue](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rescue_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈreskjuː/` · **US:** `/ˈreskjuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/rescu/rescue__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/rescu/rescue__us_2.mp3)

  - a mountain rescue team

<a id="w-2442"></a>
### 2442. [researcher](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/researcher) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈriːsɜːtʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/rɪˈsɜːrtʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resea/researcher__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resea/researcher__us_6.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - European researchers say olive oil could help prevent cancer.

  - researcher in something a leading researcher in the field of artificial intelligence

- **UK:** `/rɪˈzaɪd/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resid/reside__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resid/reside__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, रहना

<a id="w-2451"></a>
### 2451. [residence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/residence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrezɪdəns/` · **US:** `/ˈrezɪdəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resid/residence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resid/residence__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निवास, निवास स्थान, निवास की अनुमति
- **Examples:**
  - a desirable family residence for sale (= for example, in an advertisement)
  - 10 Downing Street is the British Prime Minister’s official residence.

<a id="w-2452"></a>
### 2452. [resident](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resident_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrezɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈrezɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resid/resident__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resid/resident__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निवासी
- **Examples:**
  - the town’s resident population (= not tourists or visitors)
  - to be resident abroad/in the UK
  - Tom's our resident expert (= our own expert) on foreign movies.

<a id="w-2453"></a>
### 2453. [residential](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/residential) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌrezɪˈdenʃl/` · **US:** `/ˌrezɪˈdenʃl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resid/residential__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resid/residential__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˈrezɪdjuː/` · **US:** `/ˈrezɪduː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resid/residue__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resid/residue__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-2455"></a>
### 2455. [resign](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resign) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzaɪn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resig/resign__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resig/resign__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इस्तीफा देना, सुपुर्द करना, छोड़ देना
- **Examples:**
  - She was forced to resign due to ill health.
  - resign as something He resigned as manager after eight years.
  - resign from something Two members resigned from the board in protest.
  - resign over something Some judges have threatened to resign over this issue.

<a id="w-2456"></a>
### 2456. [resignation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resignation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌrezɪɡˈneɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌrezɪɡˈneɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resig/resignation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resig/resignation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वेच्छा, इश्तीफा, इस्तीफा
- **Examples:**
  - a letter of resignation
  - There were calls for her resignation from the board of directors.
  - Further resignations are expected.
  - The defeat led to his resignation as team manager.

<a id="w-2457"></a>
### 2457. [resist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resist) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzɪst/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resis/resist__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resis/resist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रोकना, निवारण  करना, विरोध करना
- **Examples:**
  - resist something They are determined to resist pressure to change the law.
  - People naturally resist change.
  - resist doing something The bank strongly resisted cutting interest rates.
  - Residents who oppose the plans are threatening to resist.

<a id="w-2458"></a>
### 2458. [resistance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resistance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzɪstəns/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzɪstəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resis/resistance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resis/resistance__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाधा, विरोधी संस्था, विरोध
- **Examples:**
  - As with all new ideas it met with resistance.
  - resistance to somebody/something There has been a lot of resistance to this new law.
  - Resistance to change has nearly destroyed the industry.

<a id="w-2459"></a>
### 2459. [resistant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resistant)

<a id="w-2460"></a>
### 2460. [resolution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resolution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌrezəˈluːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌrezəˈluːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resol/resolution__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resol/resolution__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विश्लेषण, इरादा/संकल्प, प्रस्ताव
- **Examples:**
  - Have you made any New Year's resolutions (= for example, to give up smoking from 1 January)?
  - resolution to do something She made a resolution to visit her relatives more often.
  - I made a New Year resolution to give up smoking.

<a id="w-2461"></a>
### 2461. [resolve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resolve_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzɒlv/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzɑːlv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resol/resolve__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resol/resolve__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चय करना, समाधान करना, हल करना
- **Examples:**
  - resolve something to resolve an issue/a dispute/a conflict/a crisis
  - Attempts are being made to resolve the problem of security in schools.
  - Both sides met in order to try to resolve their differences.
  - We hope to resolve the matter as soon as possible.

### 2464. [respect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/respect_1) *(noun)*

### 2472. [restoration](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restoration) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌrestəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌrestəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resto/restoration__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resto/restoration__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मरम्मत, पुनःस्थापन, नवीनीकरण

  - for restoration The palace is closed for restoration.

<a id="w-2473"></a>
### 2473. [restore](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restore) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resto/restore__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resto/restore__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पुनः स्थापित करना, मरम्मत करना, वापस लौटाना
- **Examples:**
  - restore something The measures are intended to restore public confidence in the economy.
  - Order was quickly restored after the riots.
  - Such kindness restores your faith in human nature (= makes you believe most people are kind).
  - The operation restored his sight (= made him able to see again).

- **UK:** `/rɪˈstreɪnt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstreɪnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restraint__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restraint__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संयम, अवरोध, रोक
- **Examples:**
  - The government has imposed export restraints on some products.
  - social restraints on drinking alcohol

<a id="w-2476"></a>
### 2476. [restrict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restrict) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाधित करना, सीमित करना, परिमित/सीमित करना
- **Examples:**
  - restrict something to something Speed is restricted to 30 mph in towns.
  - We restrict the number of students per class to ten.
  - restrict something Fog severely restricted visibility.
  - Having small children tends to restrict your freedom.

<a id="w-2477"></a>
### 2477. [restriction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restriction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमा, रोक, प्रतिबन्ध
- **Examples:**
  - import/speed/travel restrictions
  - restriction on something to impose/place a restriction on something
  - The government has agreed to lift restrictions on press freedom.
  - There are no restrictions on the amount of money you can withdraw.

### 2601. [scratch](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/scratch_2) *(noun)*

### 2762. [speculate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुमान लगाना, अंदाज़  लगाना, सट्टा लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - speculate (about/on/as to something) We all speculated about the reasons for her resignation.
  - They were all speculating as to the identity of the stranger.
  - speculate why, how, etc… It is useless to speculate why he did it.
  - speculate that… We can speculate that the stone circles were used in some sort of pagan ceremony.

### 2763. [speculation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculation) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** सट्टेबाज़ी, विचार, चिंतन
- **Examples:**

<a id="w-2774"></a>
### 2774. [spoil](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/spoil_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/spɔɪl/` · **US:** `/spɔɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spo/spoil/spoil__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spo/spoil/spoil__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

- **Meaning:** असाधारण, आश्चर्यजनक, ध्यान आकर्षित करनेवाली
- **Examples:**
  - a striking feature
  - She bears a striking resemblance to her older sister.
  - In striking contrast to their brothers, the girls were both intelligent and charming.
  - The similarities between the two cases are striking.

- **UK:** `/straɪv/` · **US:** `/straɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/str/striv/strive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/str/striv/strive__us_1.mp3)

### 2899. [sufficiently](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sufficiently) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/səˈfɪʃntli/` · **US:** `/səˈfɪʃntli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/suf/suffi/sufficiently__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/suf/suffi/sufficiently__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** काफ़ी, पर्याप्त मात्रा में, पर्याप्त रूप से
- **Examples:**
  - The following day she felt sufficiently well to go to work.
  - By 1995, bald eagles had recovered sufficiently to be removed from the endangered list.

- **Examples:**

<a id="w-2942"></a>
### 2942. [sympathetic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sympathetic) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/` · **US:** `/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sym/sympa/sympathetic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sym/sympa/sympathetic__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कारुणिक, अनुकंपी, सहानुभूतिशील
- **Examples:**
  - a sympathetic listener
  - a sympathetic response
  - She was very sympathetic when I was sick.
  - sympathetic to/towards somebody I did not feel at all sympathetic towards Kate.

<a id="w-2943"></a>
### 2943. [sympathy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sympathy) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪmpəθi/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪmpəθi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sym/sympa/sympathy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sym/sympa/sympathy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहमति, सहानुभूति, समर्थन
- **Examples:**
  - sympathy for somebody to express/feel sympathy for somebody
  - I have no sympathy for Jan—it's all her own fault.
  - I wish he'd show me a little more sympathy.
  - Our heartfelt sympathy goes out to the victims of the war.

<a id="w-2944"></a>
### 2944. [symptom](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/symptom) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪmptəm/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪmptəm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sym/sympt/symptom__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sym/sympt/symptom__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लक्षण, सूचक, रोगलक्षण
- **Examples:**
  - Symptoms include a headache and sore throat.
  - If symptoms persist, consult your doctor.
  - symptom of something to experience symptoms of anxiety and depression
  - to show/develop symptoms of illness

### 2970. [testify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/testify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सिद्ध करना, प्रमाण प्रस्तुत करना, विश्वास प्रकट  करना
- **Examples:**
  - testify against somebody/something She refused to testify against her husband.
  - testify for somebody/something There are several witnesses who will testify for the defence.
  - testify about something He was summoned to testify before a grand jury about his role in the affair.
  - testify to something/to doing something Evans testified to receiving $200 000 in bribes.

<a id="w-2971"></a>
### 2971. [testimony](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/testimony) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtestɪməni/` · **US:** `/ˈtestɪməʊni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tes/testi/testimony__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tes/testi/testimony__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रमाण, गवाही
- **Examples:**
  - This increase in exports bears testimony to the successes of industry.
  - The pyramids are an eloquent testimony to the ancient Egyptians' engineering skills.
  - His thick, swollen fingers bore testimony to a lifetime of toil.

  - personality traits
  - Awareness of class is a typically British trait.

<a id="w-2991"></a>
### 2991. [transform](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/transform) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/trænsˈfɔːm/` · **US:** `/trænsˈfɔːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tra/trans/transform__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tra/trans/transform__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बदल देना, बढ़ाना, घटाना
- **Examples:**
  - transform something/somebody (into something) The photochemical reactions transform the light into electrical impulses.
  - transform something/somebody from something (into something) Materials are transformed from their original state into other forms.
  - transform (into something) I am convinced that the electric power industry is transforming.
  - transform from something The way we work, play and communicate every day has completely transformed from a decade ago.

<a id="w-2992"></a>
### 2992. [translation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/translation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/trænzˈleɪʃn/` · **US:** `/trænzˈleɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tra/trans/translation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tra/trans/translation__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थानांतरण, परिक्रमणहीन एक समान गतिविधि, भाषांतर
- **Examples:**
  - an error in translation
  - translation from something into something He specializes in translation from Danish into English.
  - translation from something The request must have lost something in the translation from the French.
  - translation into something Several books are already in translation into other languages.

- **UK:** `/trænzˈmɪʃn/` · **US:** `/trænzˈmɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tra/trans/transmission__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tra/trans/transmission__us_2_rr.mp3)

### 2998. [tribute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/tribute) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtrɪbjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈtrɪbjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tri/tribu/tribute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tri/tribu/tribute__us_1.mp3)
